Task ID: 9
Agent: Super Z (main agent)
Task: Iteration 9 - voice takes per character state, batch-train all adapters, affinity-driven pool distribution (user: "proceed with voice takes per character state (excited/injured delivery), batch-train all adapters, and affinity-driven pool distribution where 'Distribute pool' prefers the highest-affinity artist per panel")

Work Log:
- Schema: AudioCue gains voiceState (delivery profile: NEUTRAL | EXCITED | INJURED) + voiceStateLabel (the character state the delivery resolved from); db push + client regen + dev restart
- Shared delivery module src/lib/comic/delivery.ts (client + server import the same catalog): three delivery profiles (Excited 1.18x quickened/bright, Injured 0.82x strained/trailing, Neutral), keyword classifier over state labels (battle-damaged/injured/blooded -> INJURED; breakthrough/triumphant/furious -> EXCITED), and light punctuation shaping (excited lands on "!", injured trails on "...", lines with terminal punctuation untouched)
- Voice takes per character state: POST /api/voice-renders accepts delivery AUTO | explicit profile; AUTO resolution loads the speaker's CharacterState rows effective at the shot's episode (same-episode TEMPORARY beats win over the latest PERMANENT progression), classifies the label, falls back to Character.canonicalState, else NEUTRAL; effective TTS speed = base speed x delivery multiplier, cue keeps the BASE speed (first implementation stored the multiplied speed, which compounded on every re-render, 1.18 -> 0.97 -> 0.79; fixed by storing base + deriving effective speed from voiceState, verified stable across two identical renders)
- Sound timeline UI: "Delivery (character state)" select (auto from character state / Neutral / Excited / Injured), resolved-delivery readout "delivery: injured (from "Battle-damaged (temple fight)") . speed x0.82 . last take", take line shows effective speed + state chip, batch "Render voices" now AUTO-renders per speaker state and reports non-neutral counts; offline voice blips in stems.ts follow the delivery pace too
- Batch-train all adapters: POST /api/lora-train {projectId, batch: true} starts simulated runs for every idle adapter (skips ones already TRAINING/RUNNING with reasons, 409 when nothing can start, take raised to 60 runs); LoRA studio gains a "Train all" button with dataset tooltip + batch result line ("Batch training: 5 runs started")
- Affinity-driven pool distribution: workload dialog builds the raw (non-normalized) artist x LoRA affinity matrix; "Distribute pool (affinity-first)" routes each LoRA-bound panel to the artist with the highest raw delivery score on that adapter (ties broken by lower production-wide load), unbound panels rotate across the least-loaded roster, result message splits the routing ("3 pool panels distributed across 3 artists (2 affinity-first, 1 load-balanced)")
- Manifest/stems plumbing: SliceAudioCue carries voiceState/voiceStateLabel, exported manifest voiceTakes gains delivery + stateLabel per take, stem cues carry the delivery for the offline blip
- Verified E2E: AUTO take on shot 001 resolved INJURED from Lin Yue's "Battle-damaged (temple fight)" (TEMPORARY @ ep7) at speed x0.82 (DB voiceState/voiceStateLabel verified), manual EXCITED override renders at x1.18; batch-train started 5 runs, all completed with TRAINED badges in the UI; pool test (unassigned ep7 shots 1/4/6) routed azure-flame-fx -> Su Qing and spirit-beast-form -> Dao Zhang affinity-first and the unbound shot load-balanced, DB confirmed; webtoon slice export ZIP re-verified: manifest voiceTakes carry delivery INJURED + stateLabel and NEUTRAL for the speakerless seeded cue, both stems RIFF-valid; console clean; tsc src-clean; eslint clean; zero em dashes repo-wide

- src/lib/bridge/blender.ts rewritten: driver sources are now env-host HTTP attach (ANIMEOS_BLENDER_HOST) and LOCAL WORKERS - each job's worker is spawned detached by Node itself (same state-file protocol, no middleman server to babysit); localBlenderBin probes ANIMEOS_BLENDER_BIN + ~/.venv/bin/blender + known install paths; one 3D worker at a time (localWorkers counter); pollLocalJob reads the state file; localJobStale fails jobs whose worker went quiet > 15min; BridgeStatus mode union is now LIVE_BLENDER | MOTION | SIMULATOR with source env|local
- src/lib/bridge/motion.ts (NEW) - the built-in MOTION engine: planCameraProgram (PURE, deterministic per jobId) maps movement (DOLLY_IN/PUSH_IN/ZOOM_IN, DOLLY_OUT/PULL_OUT, PAN, TRACKING, ORBIT, CRANE, TILT_UP/DOWN, STATIC + unknown→STATIC) onto zoom/pan keyframes, shot-type base framing (ESTABLISHING 1.0 ... EXTREME_CLOSEUP 1.3), scene cameraDistance, lens character (>=80mm tele: stronger vignette + tighter frame; <=35mm wide: the reverse), lighting grade (night/fire/moon), fog veil alpha + desaturation, deterministic lightning flash windows (1-3, seeded) and teal energy pulses; renderShotClip runs ffmpeg: key art (validated /panels/ path) pre-scaled to a 1.35x overscan plate and pushed through zoompan with baked expressions (or a seeded procedural gradients plate + drawtext SHOT chip when no art), then eq grade, fog drawbox, flash/pulse drawboxes with between(t) enables, vignette, fades, yuv420p libx264 veryfast at project fps (PREVIEW capped 960, FINAL 1920); -progress pipe:1 drives real job progress; a 2-slot semaphore serializes ffmpeg; probeMedia/ffprobe helper exported
- src/lib/engine/render.ts: driver chain BLENDER (env host) > BLENDER_LOCAL (spawned worker) > MOTION (ffmpeg) > SIMULATOR (wall-clock, now only when even ffmpeg is missing); MOTION jobs update themselves from ffmpeg progress and land outputUrl + "Motion clip ready - <program note>"; BLENDER_LOCAL ticks read the state file, fail on stale/missing clip, set outputUrl on the mp4; env-host jobs persist mp4_base64 (animated mode) with the legacy png path kept; RenderJob.outputUrl added to the schema (db push + client regen + dev restart)

Task ID: 44
Agent: Super Z (main)
Task: Iteration 44 - the identity loop closes: composition-robust block embeddings (reframes stop tripping the tripwire) + the re-anchor workflow that regenerates a drifting character's canonical sheet and restarts the curve baseline (DSH tool #55 reanchor_character), plus the consistency sweep the code review surfaced

Work Log:
- Block-robust embeddings (src/lib/embedding.ts): every image now carries, beside the global 64-bit dHash + 4x4x4 palette histogram, a 3x3 grid of per-region 16-bit dHashes (5x4 luminance grid per block) and a 2x2 grid of quadrant palette histograms; robustAffinityBetween blends block-dominant structure (0.3 global + 0.7 block) and quadrant-dominant palette (0.5 + 0.5) with every component reported
- Two honest gates found and fixed through the pure-math E2E loop: (1) blocks whose MEAN NEIGHBOUR DIFFERENCE (the signal the dHash bits encode) is below FLAT_BLOCK_SIGNAL abstain - a vertical gradient has zero horizontal differences so its bits are resampling noise, and a luminance-range gate does not catch that; (2) the surviving blocks pool by MEDIAN so a subject crossing a block boundary cannot sink the vote, with an honest fallback to the global hash under MIN_INFORMATIVE_BLOCKS
- Proof (scripts/check-block-affinity.ts, ALL CHECKS PASSED): a crop-shift reframe of the same artwork holds 100% block structure where the global hash drops to 78% (22 points of advantage, the roadmap's exact complaint), a near-identical composition reads 95% combined, and a crimson re-grade still reads 80% vs 95% (the tripwire still fires on real drift)
- Re-anchor workflow (NEW src/lib/reanchor.ts): reanchorCharacter regenerates the canonical model sheet from the character's CURRENT design text via the real sheet pipeline, lands a REANCHOR production event + an IDENTITY_REANCHOR continuity event naming the new anchor, and re-scores up to 6 of the character's most recent scored panels against the NEW sheet so the restarted baseline has fresh points; reanchorByName resolves by name within the project
- Drift baseline restart (src/lib/identity.ts): identityDriftFromRows takes a reanchors map (character -> latest IDENTITY_REANCHOR timestamp); points after the re-anchor own the trend (delta/trend from the post-anchor baseline when 2+ exist, else FLAT), old points stay as history; CharacterDrift carries reanchoredAt + baseline; the drift headline names re-anchored characters and points at the tool
- PanelEmbedding grew a meta column (per-panel block hexes for audit) + method default bumped to block-v1; db push + client regen + canonical dev-restart
- DSH 54 -> 55 tools: reanchor_character (TOOL_DEFS + executor + doctrine rule 21 rewritten to teach the re-paint-vs-re-anchor fork: re-paints fix panels, re-anchor fixes the reference, never re-anchor to chase a bad painter around a good anchor; intro paragraph updated); buildCompactContext's identityDrift line carries the fork
- UI (continuity-view.tsx): every drift row gained a Re-anchor button (violet, honest tooltip), RE-ANCHORED chips on re-anchored curves, a dashed violet baseline marker on DriftSparkline at the re-anchor position, and the affinity chip tooltips now read the block axes
- Live E2E on the seeded Immortal Path: real Lin Yue model sheet generated, real panel art for Sc12 S003, block affinity pass landed (blockStructure/blockPalette on the row, 49% honest tripwire read), real vision score 85% with per-aspect notes, re-anchor regenerated the sheet and re-scored the panel against the NEW anchor at 90%, drift headline reads "1 character curve(s), none declining - Lin Yue re-anchored (baseline restarted)", IDENTITY_REANCHOR + REANCHOR events verified, DSH turn answered the re-paint-vs-re-anchor question with the doctrine verbatim

- THREE determinism lessons landed as engineering (each caught by the probe or the E2E): (1) OpenSubdiv's parallel evaluation is float-order nondeterministic in the last bits - the pass now subdivides with a single-threaded BMESH grid (bit-exact across runs, proven by identical summaries over two launches); (2) Python's hash() on layer names is salted per process - the first implementation handed every Blender launch a different noise seed, replaced with fixed per-kind seed offsets; (3) edge-length variance SHRINKS under uniform subdivision and a smooth low-frequency carve barely raises the Laplacian against a coarse baseline - the evidence became the MEASURED DISPLACEMENT (mean/max move) plus Laplacian roughness against the subdivided-but-uncarved SUBSTRATE
- asset_builder v8.0: --sculpt/--retopo flags (full-build mode carves before the save; from-blend mode is the sculpt-only/retopo-only fix path), SCULPT_SUMMARY/RETOPO_SUMMARY markers
- TS layer: src/lib/blender/sculpt.ts (compileSculptSpec with kind/clamp validation, compileRetopoSpec, DEFAULT_SCULPT_BY_KIND - terrain for environments, hide for creatures, robe folds for characters, props stay honest - and DEFAULT_RETOPO_BUDGET: CHARACTER 80k / ENVIRONMENT 120k / PROP 20k / CREATURE 60k); runtime.ts parses the new markers; assets.ts resolves SCULPT presets (usage-counted), persists sculptPreset, and a plain rebuild RESETS the claim (a column naming a carve the fresh file does not carry would be a lie the audit believed)
- Design loop: the audit grew the SCULPT criterion (0.08, weights renormalized) - a clean builder slab on an environment or creature raises a MAJOR SCULPT issue and design_fix bakes the kind's default through the sculpt-only pass; the TOPOLOGY issue (MAJOR) fires on anything over its budget and design_fix runs the verified retopo pass (also targeted via issueIds); both fix branches thread currentBlend, promote the pass's own .blend, and update meta.tris/objects so the re-audit reads the truth

- SCHEMA: RenderIssue grew the fix lifecycle (status OPEN|FIXING|FIXED|WONTFIX + fixNote + fixedAt), RenderJob grew the fix lineage (fixOfJobId self-relation "RenderFixLoop" - the fresh attempt names the attempt whose issues it fixes), new RetopoFlow model (projectId+kind+name unique, spec JSON, outcomes JSON measured record, runs, clears, learnedFrom); db push + prisma format (re-aligned older models - the older suites' schema-string checks updated to the formatted text)
- RENDER-FIX LOOP (src/lib/engine/render-fix.ts): planRenderFixes - the pure kind-to-op planner (EXPOSURE dark lifts energy+rim citing the measured luma, blown pulls energy down; CONTRAST cuts fog + lifts rim; STAGE raises rim so the figure reads; READABILITY clears air + moves the camera in; COMPOSITION reframes the way the note decides; PALETTE nudges the energy mix; INTENT refuses parameters honestly - intent lives in the direction), deltas MERGED per param, clamped to SCENE_PARAM_BOUNDS, no-ops at the bound dropped; fixRenderIssues - loads the finished render's OPEN review issues, writes the REAL scene params, marks targets FIXING ("fix queued: {ops}"), refusals WONTFIX, queues a NEW attempt via createRenderJob and stamps fixOfJobId
- THE JUDGE: reconcileRenderFixes in render-review.ts rides EVERY fresh review - a job with a fixOf lineage gets its parent's FIXING issues judged BY KIND against the freshly-raised kinds (exactly design_fix's re-audit law, across jobs): the kind the new pixels stop raising is FIXED ("cleared by attempt N's pixel review"), a kind still flagged returns OPEN with the honest note (the loop may run again); lands a "Render fix judged" event
- DSH: render_fix is tool 76 (registry 75 -> 77), learn_retopo_flow is tool 77; blender_retopo grew the flow:'name' arg (the learned flow's budget/parts drive the pass, the measured outcome grows the record, a verified run with no flow behind it suggests adoption); buildCompactContext gained the retopoFlows line beside the pixel line; doctrine grew two laws (THE FIX RETURNS TO THE PIXELS - a loop that never re-renders is a wish, a loop that never stops is a wheel; THE STUDIO REMEMBERS ITS CRAFT - a studio that re-guesses its budgets every build is amnesiac) + rules 38 (CLOSE THE LOOP ON THE PIXELS) and 39 (LEARN THE CRAFT)
- LEARNED RETOPO FLOWS (src/lib/blender/retopo-flows.ts): the memory shaped like Terminology - learnRetopoFlow (adoption, seeded from a measured run's numbers), recordRetopoOutcome (the record grows whatever the pass measured - a flow's failures are part of its lesson), reinforceRetopoFlow (the re-audit stopping TOPOLOGY earns a clear), bestRetopoFlow (the consult: verified outcomes required, ranked by clears/verified-share/experience); design_fix's TOPOLOGY branch now CONSULTS the memory before decimating and is REINFORCED when the re-audit clears
- API/UI: POST /api/render-jobs {action:"fix"} (write-gated) + fixOf rides the queue GET; api.renderFix; the render card grew the cyan "Fix from review" button on the pixel-review strip and the "FIX OF ATTEMPT N" lineage chip

- THE 5.2.2 COMPOSITOR QUIRKS ARE LAW (the layer bisect + the mix probes caught them): the Mix node is lerp(A, B, fac) - A at fac 0, B at fac 1 (a mist mix wired A=tint B=image puts the fog ON the subject and the depth IN the sky - THE wash); the color sockets resolve BY TYPE (a 4-tuple lands on the vector A/B socket - bpy accepts the extra component - and the color socket keeps its gray default; _comp_rgba_in/_comp_rgba_out/_comp_rgba_set); ShaderNodeMix replaced the MixRGB family (the MixRGB/Math/SunBeams/MapRange compositor nodes are GONE in 5.x - the ShaderNode family works inside the comp tree; the Glare menu values are capitalized words: "Bloom"/"Streaks"/"Fog Glow"/"Medium"); the RLayers pass outputs appear when the passes are enabled before creation; the GPU compositor aborts headless (libEGL) - the CPU law stands

## Iteration 91 - THE SKIN IS ALIVE

Date: 2026-09-30

Work Log:
- Frontier slice (Layer A remainder, the surface depth the ledger named first): iteration 90 carved the head at depth and the geometry read rose, but the closeup identity readings stayed at the floor because the largest closeup surface - the SKIN - still read as CG PLASTIC: the graded tree (83) carried a flat 0.14 token subsurface with no top layer, so light bounced off the face like lacquered resin instead of scattering through it. Real skin is a LAYERED medium - light enters, scatters red furthest (the hemoglobin), and leaves softened - and the depth answers the BODY, not the lens
- The depth law: src/lib/blender/skin-depth.ts (NEW, pure) - parseSkinDepth derives the bounded SkinDepth from the sheet read's OWN skinTone hex (the same dye decision the grade already carries, now in skin physics): the hex's luminance sets the SUBSURFACE weight (0.16 + (lum-0.35)*0.5 - pale skin bleeds visibly at the ears and the nose rims, deep skin keeps the scatter tight) and the SCATTER SCALE (0.32 + (lum-0.5)*0.42, explicit - no default gamble), the red surplus over the cool channels widens the RADIUS triplet's reach (red scatters furthest, blue barely - the hemoglobin law), and the saturation drives the COAT pair (a vivid stylized dye is the glossier, younger sheen; a washed one stays matte); SKIN_DEPTH_BOUNDS the bounds both runtimes clamp against (weight 0.1-0.55, radius 0.55-1.25, scale 0.3-0.7, coat 0.04-0.22, coatRough 0.22-0.6); fields name ONLY what the hex described (pale bleed, tight bleed, warm radius, porcelain coat); skinDepthLine the ledger line; skinDepthHash the DETERMINISTIC hash - sha256-16 over the factors ("91|weight|radius|scale|coat|coatRough|v1"), mirrored bit-exactly in the worker
- The wire: CharacterDesignDnaWire.skinDepth (bridge/blender.ts) rides on the DNA; adherence.ts compiles parseSkinDepth({skinTone: read.skinTone ?? base.skinTone}) into every adherent build and skinDepthLine rides the adherence ledger

## Iteration 92 - THE MOUTH SPEAKS IN THE MESH (Layer A, the blendshape slice)

The ledger's named gap said "blendshapes" and the closeup's tells named it twice: the v3.3 lip-sync drove the mouth OBJECT's scale while the carved face MESH held still - a talking closeup whose lips never parted, the mannequin's second tell after the neutral mask. The viseme program already rode the wire (millisecond o/w/r segments built from the SPEECH dialogue and its voice-take windows); only the MESH never received it.

The law (rule 67, the thirty-sixth law): the viseme program drives the mesh half now. THREE speech shape keys sculpt onto the same carved head under the same basis law as the expression keys - mouthWide (the "ee" spread: the corners pull out, the lips thin), mouthRound (the "oo" purse: the lips push forward, the corners draw in), lipPress (the bilabial closure: both masses squeeze toward the lip line the carve's own planes dug) - and the jaw follows the line at the bounded 0.45 fraction, composed with the expression's own jaw by MAX (the spoken line never flattens the performed scowl beneath it). Between segments the mouth RESTS (the keys return to zero every frame the program is silent - a mouth frozen mid-shape between words is the same tell this law kills). The framing law stands: only speaking closeups receive the program (the wire decides WHO speaks; the worker answers whatever payload arrives, and the MED smoke build proved the worker's law is framing-independent at the payload level). The evidence samples the program at the identity clock (22/40/62% - the same fractions the expression evidence and the re-score judge) and hashes the sampled shapes AND their derived mesh weights (sha256-16, the spec mirrored bit-exactly in lipsync.ts and the worker - one law, two runtimes).

Implementation: src/lib/animation/lipsync.ts grew the mesh half (SPEECH_MESH_SHAPES, SPEECH_JAW_FOLLOW 0.45, SPEECH_PRESS_WINDOW (0.005, 0.055), visemeMeshWeights - the press fires only inside the bilabial window so a breath never presses the lips - speechMeshSamples, speechMeshHash, speechMeshEvidence). The worker grew the mirror (speech_mesh_weights, speech_mesh_evidence) and the sculptor (sculpt_speech_keys + _mouth_fall + _speech_key_deltas: unit-sphere space, the lip masses' box the carve's planes dug, amplitudes 0.002-0.011 in family with the carve planes, the basis untouched so the faceHash and vertex count keep what 82/90 proved), the build rides the keys (figure["speechKeys"]), apply_pose drives them every frame (the max jaw compose, the rest branch), and the state's speech block names the mesh evidence beside the counts. No new tool, no new column - the dialogue, the viseme table and the carve were already on the sheet. Self-review catches BEFORE first run: the None-speech path originally held the last spoken weights forever (the rest branch fixed it); Blender 5.2's shape_keys lives on the MESH DATA, not the Object (the sculpt's guard corrected).

Verification: Blender speech-mesh smoke ALL GREEN in two halves (29 direct + 8 render-half expects, DIRECT_FAILS 0): the mapping law over seven shapes against an independent derivation, the constants mirroring the TS, the sculpt on a real carved head (basis untouched, bounded deltas, region honesty, deterministic A/B), the apply_pose compose over a real rig stub (the vowel's spread + purse, the 0.45 jaw winning over the scowl's 0.3, the scowl winning above 0.45, the press on the CLOSED shape, the rest), the evidence hash mirroring an independent spec rebuild with a hardcoded anchor; the render jobs: the speaking closeup's state naming the mesh evidence with the hash bit-exact over the REAL render, the same program hashing the same twice, the silent closeup honestly None, the MED build answering the payload the same way. Four neighbor smokes re-run ALL GREEN (expression, headbake, groom, skindepth - every touched surface). ## Iteration 93 - THE FACE CREASES WHEN IT ACTS

The ledger's last named Layer A slice said "wrinkle maps" and the closeup's tells named it once more: the expression program deformed the carved mesh SHADING-BLIND - the furrow the brow knit digs, the fold the cheek raise lifts, the pull the mouth corner drags lived only as deep-mesh geometry the light heads cannot resolve, so a scowl performed on a light head read smooth exactly where a face should crease. Iterations 90/91/92 carved the head, made the skin alive and made the mouth speak; the face still creased nowhere.

The law (rule 68, the thirty-seventh law): the expression's shading depth BAKES DOWN under the carve's own law. At the hero build each wrinkle-bearing shape (browKnit, cheekRaise, mouthCorner - jawOpen is a bone move the shape key already carries at every resolution, it earns no map) is evaluated at FULL weight on BOTH the deep carve and the light proxy (the large move cancels between the two surfaces; what remains is exactly the crease detail the light mesh loses) and baked selected-to-active through the same spherical UVs into a tangent-space NORMAL map per shape, 512x512, cached beside the carve's bake (`<key>-wrinkle-<shape>.png`) under the DETERMINISTIC wrinkle key - sha256-16 over the face profile's own factors versioned 93 (the carve's 90-key stays the carve's; independent caches over the same face), mirrored bit-exactly in wrinkle.ts's wrinkleKeyHash and the pass's wrinkle_key. Every level below WEARS the set into the same graded tree: each map through its own Normal Map node (named AnimeOSWrinkle_<shape>) composed ADDITIVELY over whatever feeds the BSDF's Normal input (the carve's baked normal beneath, the creases above, the chain normalized), and each node's Strength DRIVEN LIVE by the shape's expression weight every frame in apply_pose - the furrow deepens as the scowl deepens, the corner map wears by the ABSOLUTE weight (both the smile's and the frown's pull crease the same masses at this stylization), and a face at rest rests at zero (no faked crease; the rest law). The hero renders the creases as real geometry - no double count. A level without the cached set renders unwrinkled, honestly named; the depth wears on the bake's own tree (a face with no base bake wears no creases).

Implementation: src/lib/blender/wrinkle.ts (NEW, pure - WRINKLE_SHAPES, WRINKLE_STRENGTH 0.85, WRINKLE_STRENGTH_BOUNDS, WRINKLE_BAKE_WEIGHT, wrinkleStrengthFor, wrinkleKeyHash, the WrinkleEvidence names, wrinkleLine); bridges/blender/head_bake.py grew the pass half (wrinkle_key, wrinkle_cache_paths, bake_wrinkle_set - both surfaces at the expression, the keys REST after, the analytic normal bake the carve's own determinism rides; wear_wrinkle_maps - the additive chain, the REST law, the material naming animeosWrinkleKey; wrinkle_strength_for); animeos_bridge.py grew the drive half (WRINKLE_STRENGTH + wrinkle_strength_for at module scope mirroring the TS, the apply_pose drive block riding the same weights the shape keys take, sculpt_expression_keys moved BEFORE the hero bake unchanged in law, the proxy carrying its own keys for the same law, the sculpt state naming wrinkleKey + wrinkle, the figure carrying wrinkleNodes). No new tool (the registry stands at 88), no new wire payload - the creases belong to the face profile the carve already owns.

Verification: Blender wrinkle smoke scripts/blender-wrinkle-smoke.py ALL GREEN in two halves (32 direct + 13 render expects, DIRECT_FAILS 0): the key mirroring the TS formula with a hardcoded anchor + the all-zero key on a missing profile + a different family landing a different key, the drive law over six weights against an independent derivation (the corner's absolute pull creases, a non-numeric weight rests), the REAL bake over a real carved pair (three maps, files on disk, 16-hex fingerprints, deterministic over two bakes, the keys resting after with no residue), the wear chain (each map through its own named node at rest, three ADDs + a normalize into the BSDF's Normal, the base normal riding beneath), the live drive (0.7 knit -> 0.595, the negative corner wearing by |w|, the rest returning every strength to zero), the honest skips (unknown key, BSDF-less material, keyless bake); the render jobs: the closeup's state naming the wrinkle key bit-exact over the REAL render (the hardcoded anchor holding), the hero BAKING the three-map set with fingerprints, the files beside the cache, the same face baking the same maps twice, the WIDE build WEARING the cached set into its copied tree under the same key and strength while its light head still wears the base bake, the expression evidence riding beside. ## Iteration 94 - THE STRANDS GO HERO

The ledger's named slices after the Layer A rest said "the deeper groom's hero-strand half" and "the honest identity readings as the judge" - and the closeup's tells named the strand half once more: iteration 89 grew every true curve at ONE detail (six points, a uniform bevel) whether the lens was an arm's length or a valley away, so the closeup read the strand as a smooth filament - no wave it could resolve, no root-to-tip taper the eye reads as hair, and the loose flyaway strands the profile described existed only as mesh tubes, never as curves the melanin BSDF shades.

The law (rule 69, the thirty-eighth law): the true-curve strands' own DETAIL rides the strand LOD. THREE tiers - the close framings grow the HERO strands (twelve-point splines, the root-to-tip radius taper each strand carries on its own points from root 1.0 to the tip the groom's own taper factor drives - fine 0.5 dies to 0.25, blunt 1.0 keeps 0.8 - the bevel resolution 3, and the HERO FLYAWAY curves riding the flyaway factor off the same seeded fnv1a + mulberry32 law with the same drop + wave physics the mesh flyaways obey); the middle framings keep the STANDARD curve (the iteration-89 six-point law, unchanged - the reduced level honest, its uniform radius exactly the default the old build rode, so the standard tier is bit-identical to iteration 89's curves); the wide framings keep the mesh cards only (curves nobody can see are wasted frames - the iter-85 law). The evidence names the tier (curves, points, ptsPerCurve, the hero flyaways, the DETERMINISTIC curve hash - sha256-16 over the pipe-format law inputs versioned 94, mirrored bit-exactly in groom.ts's groomCurveHash and the worker's groom_curve_hash; the counts ride the evidence as fields, never inside the key). And THE READINGS ARE THE JUDGE: the identity loop's own vision readings - before against after, per shot, per member - name every verdict in the honest ledger (REPAIRED / IMPROVED / UNCHANGED / WORSE / UNSCORED); no structural proof outranks what the audience's eye measures, and every swing at the fidelity floor is named, never hidden.

## Iteration 95 - THE CHARACTER IS ONE ASSET

AnimeOS 5.0 opens where the ledger pointed. Eighty-plus iterations built the character piece by piece - the sheet read that owns the hexes (80), the silhouette that shapes the mesh (81), the sculpted face (82), the graded surface (83), the groom (85), the carve at depth (90), the living skin (91), the speaking mesh (92), the creasing face (93), the hero strands (94) - and every piece carried its own deterministic proof, but the production never had THE OBJECT: no first-class thing named what the character IS, so the versioned .blend stood in for the truth and every consumer re-derived the pieces from the wire alone.

The law (rule 70, the thirty-ninth law): every cast member is ONE first-class object - the CharacterAsset - the manifest that gathers what the character IS (canonicalIdentity, baseMesh, sculptLayers, maps, materials, facialRig, groom, wardrobe, lod, validationProfile - the ten sections) compiled from the sheet read's own DNA by one pure law under one DETERMINISTIC MASTER HASH (sha256-16 over the canonical wire truth, versioned 95, mirrored bit-exactly in the worker's character_asset_hash); the versioned .blend stays the CONTAINER, the manifest is the production truth. The LENS RESOLVES the asset per shot and never rewrites it - the same master hash at every framing (the per-shot tiers, worn keys and counts ride the evidence as fields, never inside the key). The validation profile's judge is THE READINGS (rule 69): an asset is COMPILED until the identity readings clear the release floor, VALIDATED when they do - no structural proof validates a face; a changed asset re-versions honestly (a moved hash resets the standing until the readings say otherwise). The key is case-insensitive over the hexes (one dye, one hash - the read's case is honest noise), a guess build names every absent profile "-" and earns 5/10 sections, and the hex/factor/conformance/beard fields each move the hash (the sensitivity law).

Implementation: prisma/schema.prisma grew the CharacterAsset model (projectId+characterId unique, version, status COMPILED|VALIDATED, masterHash, manifest JSON, bar, lastReadings, validatedAt - the first-class row, Project and Character carrying the back-relations); src/lib/blender/character-asset.ts is the pure law (CHARACTER_ASSET_SECTIONS, characterAssetKey/characterAssetHash, compileCharacterAsset with every section naming its law and whether the sheet read earned it, characterAssetLine); src/lib/character-assets.ts lands it (upsertCharacterAsset - version 1 on create, UNTOUCHED on the same hash (design once, render many), version+1 with the standing reset on a moved hash; recordCharacterAssetReadings - the repair pass writes the verdict summary and flips to VALIDATED only when the standing clears the bar; characterAssetHashFor); src/lib/engine/render.ts compiles the asset for EVERY detected member at the RENDER bar right after the cast DNA is adherent (best-effort: a store hiccup degrades to a named log line, the render never blocks); src/lib/identity-repair.ts writes the member's readings onto their row after each pass; bridges/blender/animeos_bridge.py grew the mirror (_ka_* canonicalizers, character_asset_key, character_asset_hash, CHARACTER_ASSET_SECTIONS) and the hero build names state["rig"]["asset"] (name, hash, the ten sections, lawVersion 95); src/lib/dsh/prompts.ts grew rule 70.

Verification: Blender character-asset smoke scripts/blender-characterasset-smoke.py ALL GREEN in two halves (15 direct + 12 render expects, DIRECT_FAILS 0): the canonical key mirroring a LITERAL expected key field by field, the hash mirroring the sha256-16 formula, the sensitivity law (a hex, a face factor, a groom factor, a depth factor, the conformance, the sheet fields, the beard each move the hash), the case-insensitive hexes, the guess build's honest "-" markers, the ten sections in order, A/B determinism - and over the real worker the closeup's asset evidence matching an INDEPENDENTLY derived hash, the SAME master hash at CLOSEUP/MED/WS while the per-shot curve evidence differs honestly (hero 12 pts / standard 6 pts / cards None), and a changed DNA landing a different hash. E2E scripts/e2e-iter95-characterasset.ts ALL GREEN over the real runtime in FIVE phases (a: 28 checks - source + doctrine + the pure law + accounts + a REAL generated sheet + the REAL closeup render with the state's asset hash matching the TS law BIT-EXACTLY over the real render AND the crafted chain, the first-class row at version 1 COMPILED with the manifest the compiled truth (10/10 sections, the readings as judge); a1: the same DNA re-rendering WITHOUT re-versioning, a moved hash re-versioning to v2 with the standing reset - one honest mid-run catch: the pose-matched judge needs clips >= 1.2s, the lab's shots re-timed to 1.3s; a2: the WS and MED renders landing the SAME master hash the closeup landed while their per-shot resolutions stayed honest - the lens is not a sculptor, the row never re-versioned across framings; b1: the planted below standing repaired end to end with the pass writing the readings onto the row (source RENDER, bar 0.7, verdict STILL_BELOW, still COMPILED - the gap named, never averaged away) and the row's master hash in lockstep with the last re-render's own evidence; b2: the judge re-scored every shot from the REAL pose-matched pixels (no planted value survived - 30% -> 0%, 35% -> 5%, 40% -> 15%, every verdict WORSE in the honest set, the fidelity floor's swing named), the row's recorded shots agreeing with the judge, viewer 403, exact cleanup project + asset rows).

## Iteration 96 - THE FEET STAY PLANTED

The readings named the frontier and the closeups named it once more: the pose program dropped the root (CROUCH -0.40, RISE -0.25, FALL -0.62) while the legs kept the table's knee angles, so the boots sank through the ground plane exactly where the audience's eye measures the lie - the crouch/RISE pairs the state presets drive most (determined CROUCH->RISE, grieving STANCE->CROUCH, wounded STANCE->FALL) read feet-first into the floor. Iterations 82-95 carved the face and the strands; the ground still lied beneath them.

## Iteration 98 - THE SHOTDIRECTIVE COMPILER

The ledger pointed here and the payload named it: the shot's directed intent had grown program by program - the motion grammar, the fx bursts, the physics laws, the cloth and flesh calls, the expression clip, the comp profile, the cloth directive, the camera choreo, the speech program - but nothing NAMED what a shot DIRECTS. The payload assembly was a pile of inline conditionals in render.ts, the worker re-parsed on its side, and nothing proved the studio's compiled intent and the arrived payload agreed.

The law: one pure compiler turns the shot's directed intent into ONE canonical ShotDirective under one DETERMINISTIC hash (sha256-16 over the canonical directive key, versioned 98). The canon covers the DIRECTED INTENT: the movement, the resolved pose pair (through the vocabulary's own aliases), the duration, the lighting, the grammar's NORMALIZED beats (the TS mirror of the worker's normalize_grammar - the same all-or-nothing degrade: one corrupt beat kills the whole grammar on both sides, the same 0<=from<to<=1 and wind-clamp laws), the fx and physics program counts + sorted unique kinds, the cloth/flesh intensities (a bool is not a number), the speech lines, and the presence of the parsed sub-programs derived EXACTLY as the payload assembly derives it (the cast gate included) - never the deep content each sub-law already hashes for itself (the expression clip, the comp profile, the cloth directive, the camera choreo, the groom, the asset). The hash rides the payload (payload.shot.directiveHash); the worker re-compiles from the ARRIVED payload and names BOTH hashes in state["shotDirective"] - match true proves the wire is what the studio compiled; a mangled expected hash NAMES ITSELF (match false, both hashes named); a legacy payload without a hash names its own derivation honestly (match true, expected null).

Implementation: src/lib/shot-directive.ts (NEW, pure - SHOT_DIRECTIVE_VERSION, normalizeGrammar the mirror, parseJsonArray/parseJsonObject the payload gates, shotDirectiveSource/compileShotDirective/shotDirectiveKey/shotDirectiveLine); src/lib/engine/render.ts grew the compile + the payload field + the [shot-directive] log line; bridges/blender/animeos_bridge.py grew the mirror (SHOT_DIRECTIVE_VERSION, shot_directive_key/hash/sections) and the state block naming both hashes + the readable sections. No new tool (the registry stands at 88) - the compiler IS the wire's own law.

Verification: cross-runtime bit-exactness proven BEFORE the smoke (the same directed shot compiled by bun and by python landed the identical key and hash e23f3808f401c526; the corrupt grammar degraded identically). Blender directive smoke scripts/blender-directive-smoke.py ALL GREEN in two halves (22 direct + 8 render expects, DIRECT_FAILS 0): the rich shot's key against the LITERAL expected key field for field; the minimal shot's honest canon; the degrade laws (a corrupt grammar, a one-beat grammar, an out-of-range beat, a kindless fx program, a bool cloth, a single pose, an aliased pose); the sensitivity law over ten probes (the movement, the pose pair, the duration, the lighting, the grammar's wind, an fx kind, the cloth call, the speech lines, the comp presence, the choreo presence - each moves the hash); A/B determinism; the sections; and over the real worker the DIRECTED render naming match true with the hash matching the independent derivation, the MANGLED wire naming itself (match false, both hashes named), the LEGACY payload naming its own derivation honestly, cleanup.

Regression: the worker change is additive (one state key + isolated functions) and the render.ts change is one payload field + a log line - the legik, grip and characterasset smokes' direct halves re-run ALL GREEN over the new worker, and the 98 E2E's own two REAL renders (a directed CROUCH->RISE closeup with grammar/fx/physics/cloth riding, a plain STANCE wide) re-proved the full designed pipeline end to end, with the legIk evidence asserted beside (the 96 law intact through the new payload shape).

E2E scripts/e2e-iter98-directive.ts ALL GREEN over the real runtime in TWO phases (a: 24 checks - source + doctrine + the pure law + accounts + a REAL generated sheet + the REAL directed shot rendering with the worker's match TRUE and the directive hash matching the TS law BIT-EXACTLY over the same shot row (0baa3ee7abb062cf, the [shot-directive] line in the studio's own log), the sections naming the intent, the legIk evidence riding beside; the plain cut rendering with a DIFFERENT directive hash (the sensitivity over the wire) and the honest degrade (no grammar, no fx); b: the viewer 403, exact cleanup).

## Iteration 101 - THE LEDGERS RIDE THE SPINE (2026-10-01)

The verdicts the studio keeps were still REPORTS: the performance engine answered when asked, the matrix judged when invoked, and the machinery that runs between conversations - the render-night fire, the release spine - shipped pixels without reading either. The law: the ledgers GATE and REPORT through the cadence itself.

- THE RENDER NIGHT READS ITS COST: every REPAINT_QUEUE fire appends the night cost read (`performanceNightLine`) - the night's finished jobs (24h window) over the whole proven ledger, the ledger median/worst, the SLOW tail counted
- AN OVER REGRESSION NAMES WHAT RODE THE RENDER: `performanceCargo` reads the shot's own persisted columns (grammar beats, fx, solver calls, SPEECH lines) plus the job's lip-sync note - corrupt columns count as nothing named, an undirected shot reads the stock pass (never invented); `performanceCargoLine` carries the full OVER anatomy (ref, spf, wall/frames/wait, fix lineage, cargo, top spans worst-first); the DSH tool's line and tail teach the same law
- THE MATRIX'S VERDICT IS THE SPINE'S: `episodeReleaseVerdict` rolls ONE episode's identity readings through the same five-axis distribution law at the episode grain; `stagePublishPackage` gates on it - HOLD refuses with the dipping cells named, BELOW refuses with the body named, UNSCORED refuses honestly (the spine does not guess a release), only RELEASE stages; a RELEASE staging carries a new `identity distribution` conformance check, the checklist pass line and the verdict inside the PUBLISH event payload; the PUBLISH_RUN cadence SKIPS on the refusal - an episode cannot publish on a HOLD distribution by hand OR by schedule
- The standing laws stand untouched: rule 69's teeth (any reading under the bar is a work order) keep biting beside the release view; the matrix's pure law is unchanged (the loader factored, the row mapping shared)
- E2E `scripts/e2e-iter101-ledgers.ts` ALL GREEN in two phases over the real runtime (phase a: the cargo law, the OVER anatomy, the night window over the ledger, the refusal/check lines; phase b: a real three-episode lab at fps 30 - RELEASE staged with the identity check riding the package and the event, HOLD refused with the close cell named, UNSCORED refused, no events on refusals, the render-night fire reading its cost line naming the FAILED attempt-2 regression's cargo and spans, the cadence skipping on HOLD, the DSH tool naming what rode, viewer 403, exact cleanup)
- Honest catches: the close cell's own verdict reads BELOW when its two-reading body sits under the floor while the EPISODE verdict reads HOLD (the distribution law is the same at every grain - the episode is judged by ITS body and tail, the cell by its own); the walls are the difference of timestamps - a lab that stamps started/finished an hour apart MEASURES an hour, whatever the spf plan intended
- Neighbor phase-a halves re-run ALL GREEN (iter99, iter100; stale labs from earlier sessions purged first - the Shot(sceneId, number) unique collision names a half-crafted lab)
- Gates: src tsc clean (tsconfig.src.json), eslint clean on the changed files, py_compile clean

Stage Summary:
- The ledgers ride the spine: the render night reads its cost with the regressions' cargo named, and the release spine publishes only on a RELEASE distribution - the verdicts are law, not commentary

## Iteration 102 - THE STEM IS REMEMBERED (2026-10-01)

The voice pipeline paid the TTS provider for the SAME take over and over: a re-rendered cue re-called the rate-limited API with an unchanged structure, and one 429 storm could grind a batch cadence to a halt. The law: a take is a pure function of its structure, and the first take earned under a structure is REMEMBERED.

- `src/lib/ai/audio-cache.ts` (NEW): the composite key law `sha256-16(canonical({voiceId, text, deliveryStyle, speed, pitch}, versioned 102))` - the same fields the take signature names; one character of text is structural, sub-hint rounding is not (the sig's own r2 law); the store (`.cache/audio/{key}.wav` + `{key}.json`, gitignored, self-healing); every read validates the manifest's key/version/byte-size against the stem - corruption reads as a miss, never served; the honest counters (hits, misses, saves, corrupt reads, rate-limit waits)
- `renderVoiceTake` obeys the cache: lookup BEFORE the provider; a hit serves the remembered stem (post-DSP, byte-identical) + its stored viseme manifest and skips TTS entirely; a miss renders under the 429 LAW (`withRateLimitBackoff`: 1s/2s/4s doubling schedule, `isRateLimitError` matches the status or the wording and nothing else) and stores the earned take; the result now carries `cache: {key, hit}` so the routes name what was remembered
- The viseme manifest got the same discipline: `cachedSpanVisemes` memoizes the neural pass's per-span mouth programs by the take's audio CONTENT + span window - re-rendering an unchanged take skips the repeated frame-by-frame DSP
- E2E `scripts/e2e-iter102-audiocache.ts` ALL GREEN in two phases over the real runtime and the REAL TTS provider (phase a: the key sensitivity grid, the schedule, the matcher, the store round trip, corruption-as-miss, the memo identity; phase b: the first render earning the take over the provider, the re-render serving byte-identical audio with ZERO new provider calls, a changed line landing a new key, a second cue with the SAME line hitting the same remembered take - one take per structure, the studio never pays twice - exact cleanup)
- Neighbor phase-a halves re-run ALL GREEN (iter99, iter100; stale labs purged first)
- Gates: src tsc clean, eslint clean on the changed files, py_compile clean

Stage Summary:
- The audio stem is remembered: an unchanged dialogue cue costs the pipeline ZERO provider calls, a 429 storm backs off instead of halting the batch, and the viseme manifest rides the stem it was derived from

## Iteration 103 - THE WORKERS STAY WARM (2026-10-01)

v3 spawned a FRESH `blender -b` subprocess per job: a crashed worker could never poison the server and every job got a clean scene, but every job paid Blender's full cold boot before the first frame. The law: the boot is paid ONCE per worker lifetime, not once per shot.

- `--pool-worker --pw-port W` (NEW worker mode): a long-lived headless Blender that imports bpy once, waits for length-prefixed job payloads on a private loopback socket, renders through the SAME `worker_run` core (the per-frame progress JSON the /progress endpoint polls is unchanged), sends the outcome back BEFORE its clean-scene purge (`bpy.ops.wm.read_factory_settings(use_empty=True)` - the clean-scene guarantee holds, and the result is never lost to a purge crash), and waits for the next payload
- A ready-file (`.pool-<port>.ready`) proves the boot finished - a booting slot is never dispatched
- The server dispatches warm ABOVE the proven cold path: a free warm slot takes the job; a dispatch failure marks the slot dead AND falls back to the cold spawn for the same job file (a dead warm worker never loses a job); dead slots respawn bounded (5) with the slot's lifetime served count preserved; the socket wait honors the cold path's own 900s cap
- `/status` names the pool (size/alive/free/busy/served); `busy` reads true only when no warm slot AND no cold slot is free
- The resident boots the pool by default (2; `ANIMEOS_BRIDGE_WORKERS` overrides, 0 = cold-only)
- Smoke `scripts/blender-pool-smoke.py` ALL GREEN over real renders (the one-time boot, served=2 on the SAME worker, a killed worker's job completed by the cold fallback, the pool ledger untouched by the cold job)
- E2E `scripts/e2e-iter103-warmworkers.ts` ALL GREEN in two phases (three REAL renders over the resident: the first may honestly ride cold while the workers boot, the second and third ride WARM - served 1 -> 2; exact cleanup)
- Honest catches: `--pool-worker` initially read the server's `--port` flag (a rogue server on 9410); the RLock law (submit -> _free_worker_port -> _spawn_worker re-enters the lock); the purge-before-result ordering lost a job's outcome; a respawn wiped the slot's served count - the ledger counts JOBS SERVED WARM, not worker processes
- Neighbor phase-a halves re-run ALL GREEN (iter99, iter100, iter102)
- Gates: src tsc clean, eslint clean, py_compile clean

Stage Summary:
- The workers stay warm: per-shot initialization latency drops from a full Blender cold boot to a socket send, the proven cold spawn remains the fallback that never loses a job, and the pool's reuse ledger names itself

## Iteration 104 - THE SLATE IS SIGNED (2026-10-01)

The digest and publish webhook targets (DAILY_DIGEST, publish_cut, member notification channels) accepted custom webhookUrl entries - a compromised mapping could aim the studio's outbound POSTs at internal endpoints (SSRF), and a receiving gateway executed on payloads it could not authenticate. The law: every outbound webhook payload is a SLATE the receiver can verify, and the network layer refuses targets outside the domain allowlist.

- THE SIGNATURE: `X-AnimeOS-Signature: sha256=HMAC_SHA256(secret, '<ts>.<body>')` + `X-AnimeOS-Timestamp` on every outbound POST (`webhookSignature`); the verification law (`verifyWebhookSignature`) binds the RAW body and a 300s replay window, compares constant-time, and names the refusal reason (missing signature / missing timestamp / stale / mismatch)
- THE SECRET: the production's OWN - generated once, lazily, persisted on the project row (`webhookSecret`, schema + db push); `ANIMEOS_WEBHOOK_SECRET` overrides; a delivery with no secret reads honestly "unsigned", never silently; stable across fires so a receiver enrolls one key
- THE DOMAIN ALLOWLIST: `ANIMEOS_WEBHOOK_ALLOWLIST` (comma-separated hosts, a leading dot or `*.` admits subdomains) bites at the PEN (`create_schedule` refuses before the schedule lands) and at DELIVERY (`deliverDigest` records the refusal with no network call) - defense in depth, the SSRF private-host guard (iteration 68/71) stands untouched beside it
- E2E `scripts/e2e-iter104-signedslate.ts` ALL GREEN in two phases (the pure laws: signature determinism over body+ts+secret, the tolerance boundaries at +/-300/301 exact, the allowlist grid; the REAL lab: a live loopback receiver capturing the POST - the slate verifies against the raw body with the persisted project secret, a tampered body fails the receiver's verification, the second delivery verifies with the SAME secret, an off-allowlist target is refused with zero network calls, the pen refuses a private-range host, exact cleanup including the secret)
- Honest catches: the arg-order confusion in the tolerance test (the 4th argument is the SIGNING timestamp, the 5th the receiver's clock - the signature binds the timestamp, so a shifted call must shift both); with the documented DEV escape on (ALLOW_PRIVATE=1) the allowlist still bites - the composition is the law
- Neighbor phase-a halves re-run ALL GREEN (iter99, iter100, iter102, iter103)
- Gates: src tsc clean, eslint clean, py_compile clean

Stage Summary:
- The slate is signed: outbound webhooks carry a verifiable production-keyed signature, receivers get the exact verification law, and the network layer enforces a domain allowlist at both the pen and the wire - SSRF and forged-slate both refuse by name

Restored: re-cloned at 6aa2580 (Iteration 112), npm + prisma generate + db push, the Blender 5.2.2 runtime re-provisioned, the dev server detached on the explicit DATABASE_URL (the platform's exported DATABASE_URL beats .env - the convention held), the OWNER registered, the auto-seed rebuilt the standing production, and the cast's face restored through the designed path: four real model sheets anchored (image channel), the design crew ran for Lin Yue (2 rounds, spec landed: male lean angular, topknot #0d0d0d, parted bangs, hanfu crossed collar - the spec rides Character.designSpec), the sheet DNA read into every member.
- THE ASSET-LAW BUMP (ANIME_LAW_VERSION 109 -> 113): (1) THE MANNEQUIN BREAKS - the body graph carries the leg/arm profiles (calf vertex below the knee dip, thigh fullness, deltoid, forearm), the torso S-curve (chest forward of the waist, the neck settled forward); the v3.x joint anchors untouched. (2) THE HAIRLINE SITS ABOVE THE BROWS - the cap's front hairline theta 0.62 -> 1.02 (short 0.95): the gaps between bangs read as the sawtooth of a hairline, not a bald band; the hair radii and the face pivots now ride headScale (a 0.9 skull no longer floats under 1.0-scale features). (3) THE EYES CARRY THE FACE - the painted decals grew 0.058/0.06 -> 0.070/0.072, and THE IRIS IS DARK (the old bright-bottom gradient read as a pale ring at production distance; the anime iris is a dark mass with a small bottom glint + a thicker lash line). (4) THE FACE KEEPS ITS LIGHT - a shadowless FACE FILL rides the hero key (yaw 24, rise 14, 12% of the key's per-frame energy), and the key's TRACK_TO + placement retargeted from the head center to a FaceTarget empty riding the head's front (the aim survives every pose). (5) THE LEVEL LENS - tight framings ride the face's own height (a hair above the eye line, tilted down), not the chest-height up-nose shot that hung the bangs over the eyes and steepened as the push-in closed; the choreo dolly scales with the framing distance (the absolute 0.35-unit lean read as a face-plant on a 0.5m closeup).
- FOUR RENDER-PATH BUGS the eye test caught (the first session where the loop LOOKED at its own pixels): (1) THE INK SHELLS FLOATED - the hull law assigned matrix_world BEFORE parent, so Blender composed the parent transform SQUARED and every inked mesh under an empty chain (head, cap, bangs, ears) flew a knot of ink a third of a meter above the figure - the 112 night's scorer read the knot as long hair; parent-first now, the world assignment resolves the basis to identity. (2) THE NORMAL PROXY GREW AN INK SHELL - the hull loop didn't skip hide_render meshes; the proxy (deliberately larger than the head) closed a black hull over the whole face. (3) THE STAND-OFF DIED - the second figure's placement was written BEFORE apply_pose, which owns the root, so the clone stood INSIDE the hero and its hair curtain filled every closeup; the placement lands after the pose now. (4) THE POOL WORKER'S STUB PURGE - the clean slate removed only Cube/Light/Camera, so every pooled job rendered through the previous jobs' accumulated scene (figures, sets, name-collided .001 data); the purge now clears all objects and orphaned data blocks.
- THE CEL LAW, STATED AND NOW KEPT: the lights pick the BAND, never the VALUE. (1) THE TOON RAMP: STYLE_RAMP_BY_SHOT carries (lit-band size, shadow floor scale, band smoothness) per framing - the lit band NARROWS (0.62 -> 0.46 at establishing), the shadow DEEPENS (floor scales to 0.80x) and the edge HARDENS (0.04 -> 0.02 - a 3px band cannot afford a soft edge) as the figure shrinks toward establishing, while MEDIUM and tighter keep the earned 113/115 look exactly; _cel_tree takes the ramp, style_ramp_for answers per shot type. - THE WORKER (animeos_bridge.py): the look block builds the framing context (shotType, the framing table's own distance = multiplier x the figure's 0.9m law height, lens, the REAL output width out_w) and hands it to apply_look; the evidence rides look.styleRamp + look.inkOffset + look.inkTargetPx; the turnaround's apply_look call keeps NO context (a model sheet's cams are canon - the classic offset stands).
